NOTIFICATION
Islamabad, the 18tr February, 2025.
S.RO. 168(Iy2025. - In exercise of the powers conferred by section 23 of the DrugRegulatory Authority of Pakistan Act, 20 1 2 QOil of 20 I 2), read with section 43 of the Drugs Act,1976 ()OOil of 1976), the Drug Regulatory Authority of Pakistan, with the prior approval of theFederal Govemment, is pleased to direct that in the Drugs (Research) Rules, 1978, the followingfurther amendments shall be made, the same having been previously published vide Notification,dated the 2l st day of February, 2023, as required by sub-section (3) ofthe said section 43, namely:-
ln the aforesaid Rules,-(l) in rule 2, in sub-rule (1),-
(A) the existing clauses (a) and (aa) shall be re-numbered as (ac) and (ae),respectively;
(B) before clause (ae), re-numbered as aforesaid, the following uew clausesshall be inserted, namely:-
"(a) "Act" shall have the same meaning as assigned thereto in the DRAPAct;
(aa) "Authority'' shall have the same meaning as assigned thereto in theDRAP Act;
(ab) "clinical trial" or "clinical study'' means any investigation in humansubjects intended to discover or verify the clinical, pharmacologicalor other pharmacodynamics effects of an investigational product orto identifu any adverse reactions to any investigational product or tostudy absorption, distribution, metabolism and excretion of aninvestigational product with a view of ascertaining its safety andefficacy;"; and
(C) after clause (ac), inserted as aforesaid, the following new clause (ad) shallbe inserted, namely:-
(ad) *DRAP Act" means the Drug Regulatory Authority of Pakistan Act,2012CC{rof 2012);"; and
(D) in clause (b), for the word "Fund", occurring for the first time, the"Central Research Frmd" shall be substituted;
